**Dependencies (AR3-25).** BA-06 takes normative content from four registry entries: BA-01 (the contract), BA-03 (the sealed Selective scope: the q2 domain of section 11 comes from the scope row `SEL-PLANTA`, the classes `SH-P` and `SH-FP` of section 10.2 exist because `SEL-PLANTA` is IN, and the lateral branch is out of the inventory, D-11), BA-05 (the trace source T-4 of section 7 is a `SCOPED_RECORD_COMPARISON` (BA-05 V5 section 4.1): its scope is the read-set records of the kind baseline, for Selective those of BA-08 V6 section 6, plus the `ENUMERATION` records of the covered containers, and its coverage limit is part of the declared limit (1) of the trace; BA-05 V5 defines no full-database fingerprint) and BA-07 (the manifest and custody procedure: section 9 item (2), to which section 1 and the section 12 row `INVENTORY_REVIEW_RECORDS` point, takes from it the condition under which a learning run may enter the learning corpus, the run-start anchor `IN_USE.runStartAnchor`, its `h` and the use preconditions U1 to U3 (BA-07 V6 sections 6.1 and 7), with the order "`REVIEWED` before the first `LEARNING` run" that BA-07 V6 section 5.1 states and its verification checks; the edge BA-06 -> BA-07 is acyclic: BA-07 depends only on BA-01 and precedes BA-06 in the seal order). The member list of that scope is written before the first observation of each dry run (BA-05 V5 section 4.1 item 1), so the citation of BA-08 V6 section 6 is a mention, like the fixtures, and not a dependency (BA-08 depends on BA-06). Names of fixtures (BA-08) and scenarios (BA-04), the slot `PARAM-02` (BA-10), and the characterization build and the product build (defined by AR3-01 and AR4-06; schema fields in BA-04 V6) are mentions, not dependencies. The evidence files are bound inside this artifact (section 14); that they exist at their bound hashes is a prerequisite with a before-candidate gate (header `PREREQUISITES` (2); AR3-25; R2:BA06V4-N03). **V6.** The dependencies are unchanged; the cited versions are BA-03 V5, BA-05 V5 and BA-07 V6. BA-03 V5 declares the legacy blank logical name inside the Selective scope as a legacy state (FXB-06), on which the `SH-LAY-ADDED` fixture of section 10.2 rests; the BA-07 reading of the run-start anchor used by section 9 item (2) is accepted by AR5-06 (a).

## 1. Two-stage review (V2.4)

| Stage | Object | Result | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| **E1 — design / static** | the **design-time inventory**: the reused product path at a bound SHA **plus** the operations required by the seam contract | `STATIC_REVIEWED` when conditions 1, 2, 5, 6, 7 of V2.2 PA-11 hold and the dynamic-trace plan of conditions 3, 4 is approved: **APPROVED together on the V4 bytes (AR4-12, section 217)**, carried to the V5 bytes at the bound SHA `3375aadb` (AR5-01). **V6 (AR6-01, AR6-03):** at `BOUND_PRODUCT_SHA` `95690c28` the inventory is re-derived (section 3.1) and the approval carries **per class** only where the Architect's exact delta check of these V6 bytes (AR5-02; check `BA06-C14`) shows the class without change (section 3.4); `OC-IMPORT` is re-reviewed in full, `OC-REF-TOP` and `OC-ENVELOPE` are confirmed, `OC-PREP-READ` and `OC-VERIFY-READ` are re-reviewed per AR5-03 | **baseline** (this artifact) |
| **E2 — implemented-seam dynamic trace** | the implemented seams, every seam entry point and every plan-shape class, in non-governing dry runs on the characterization build in the AR3-01 mode (section 7), under the declared limits (1) to (4); limit (4), the relation to the product build, is the one AR4-12 condition (1) requires | `REVIEWED` (complete) | **before the first `LEARNING` run** (any governance classification); needs the seams (implementation) and host work |

An operation added by the implemented seams after the design-time review reopens the review of its operation class (V2.4). The `REVIEWED` record and any reopened class review are execution-phase records kept under the custody of BA-07 (note `V24-N2`). **V4 (AR3-22, landing of `V24-N2`, finding V24R2-03):** V24-N2 says these records are "cited by the `LEARNING` entry of the EVM". That citation is the EVM-level field `INVENTORY_REVIEW_RECORDS` of section 12 (the hashes of the `REVIEWED` record and of every reopened class-review record), and the `EVM_FROZEN` pin record cites that field. **V5 (R1:BA06V4-N02, R2:BA06V4-N04):** the field is written when the EVM record is first created (`EVM_STATUS = DRAFT`, after the learning micro-scenarios, V2.1 27.2 step 3); the order "`REVIEWED` before the first `LEARNING` run" is shown by the learning records and by the BA-07 run-start anchors of the learning runs (section 9, item (2)), not by the field.

Mitigations: `B1b` swept the API surface of all of `src/RackCad.Plugin` independently of the chain; the critic's own search found the RACKEDITAR "Insertar" entry (D-01) and U-36 (`found_by` CRITIC, 3.1); the contract part was compiled from verbatim fragments (AR2-37). Check `BA06-C10` verifies these framing facts in the published task texts. **V6 product part (AR6-03; PA-11 condition 5):** the rows changed or added at `95690c28` come from the independent blind recompile `I-52-ct21d-ba06-v6-blind-recompile.json` (section 14): a compiler that did not write BA-06 and does not review it, whose instructions (recorded verbatim in its header) forbade reading BA-06 V5 section 3 and the Gate 2 assessment, compiled from the source at `95690c28` the ordered operations of the three product routes that create Selective views of a new logical rack (R1 RACKSELECTIVO/RS, R2 RACKCAD/RK, R3 RACKEDITAR 'Insertar'): 689 operations, with lock, transaction and a mutating flag, and without operation classes. ### 2.1 Delta of `main` from the V5 bound SHA to `BOUND_PRODUCT_SHA` (AR2-46; V6: AR6-01, AR5-12, AR5-13)

**V5 record (superseded as the binding, kept as history).** V5 was bound to `3375aadb` and assessed the delta to `1304101d` (AUTH-15-C1: only `Systems/Shared/RackDefinitionCreator.cs`, the overload-A envelope validation no longer requires `Name`, and the doc comment of `RackDefinitionCreationResult.cs`).

**V6 (AR6-01).** Every static fact of this artifact is bound to `BOUND_PRODUCT_SHA` = `95690c28dc6268e61dff32a0cbc33cc9fde3d47f`, the tip of `main` at the reconciliation of decisions section 222; the branch was rebased on it and `git diff 95690c28 HEAD -- src tests` is empty, so the static facts describe code the branch contains. The delta `3375aadb..95690c28` touches 89 files of `src` (AUTH-15-C1, I-55, I-60). Its consequences for this inventory (Gate 2 evidence, dimension RELINK-0605; per-row classifications in section 3.1.1): only RACKSELECTIVO/RS still reaches `SystemBlockWriter.CreateBlock`, whose lines 18-40 are textually unchanged; RACKCAD/RK (Selective) and RACKEDITAR 'Insertar' create through the I-55 batch chain (rows U-42 to U-49; U-50 is the 'Insertar' edit chain that runs first); the placement and cleanup rows moved inside `BlockPlacement.cs` and the cleanup gained triggers (U-28 to U-36); RACKPROYECTAR is a new production caller of AUTH-15 overload A and is a **precedent only** (AR6-04, section 8). The seam-write primitives are byte-identical (`LateralHeaderDrawer`, `LayerHelper`, `RackBlockData`, `BlockLibraryImporter`, `BlockLibraryDatabaseCache`, `AutoCadLibraryBlockQuery`). **No evidence is transferred** by a matching name (AR2-38, P-8). **Dated contract facts (AR5-13, AR6-05):** the facts of V2.1 15.1 that this artifact restates (F-1: one creation site that also imports; F-2: no caller-owned seam for frontal and planta) are contradicted at `95690c28` by rows U-42 to U-45 and by section 8; CT-21D V2.6 re-dates them, and no normative clause changes (header `PREREQUISITES` (3)).

**Identity table (AR5-12).** The git blobs of every file that the inventory, the residuals, the quantity sources or the precedent cite, at both SHAs. The rows whose role starts with "route" are this artifact's part of the run-preparation precondition of AR5-12: before a run, every (path, blob) pair of the reused product route that BA-03, BA-06 and BA-08 cite at the bound SHA must equal the pair at the source SHA of the build under test, or the delta is recorded and processed (a change of the design inventory is a new BA-06 and a new baseline); the precedent rows are outside that set. ## 3. Design-time inventory (V6: re-derived at `95690c28`)

**Composition (BA06V3-N04; V6).** The design-time inventory is: the reused product path at `95690c28` (3.1: U-01..U-41, U-26x and, new in V6, U-42..U-50); the compiled contract rows C-01..C-203 (3.2), with the V4 amendment of C-98 and the V6 amendment of C-72 (AR5-03) marked in the rows; the supplementary contract rows X-01..X-05 (3.2.5; X-05 is new in V6, AR5-03); the operation classes (3.3); the residuals (section 5); and the EVM schema (section 12). The difference logs (3.2.2, 3.2.3, 4.1, 4.4), the ambiguity list (3.2.4), the comparator's proposed texts, the contract questions (3.2.6), the relink ledger (3.1.1) and the per-class carry table (3.4) are records of the review, not inventory rows.

### 3.1 Reused product path at `BOUND_PRODUCT_SHA` `95690c28` (V6: re-derived, AR6-03)

Paths of the repository at `95690c28` (`BOUND_PRODUCT_SHA`). `found_by`: `A` call-graph, `B` API sweep, `CRITIC` found only by the critic (the V2 union at `3375aadb`, evidence `critic_ops` T1); **V6:** `RC` = the blind recompile at `95690c28` (section 4.4), cited on every row whose cells changed or that is new. Routes: **R1** = RACKSELECTIVO/RS (`DrawSelectiveView` -> `ViewBlockDraw` -> `SystemBlockWriter.CreateBlock` + `BlockPlacement.PlaceAndReport`); **R2** = RACKCAD/RK Selective (the I-55 batch chain: `RackViewBatchExecution.Run` -> `RackViewBatchDriver.Place` -> `RackViewPlacement.PlaceSelective`, i.e. `HeaderRequirements.Evaluate` + `HeaderMaterializer.Create` -> `SystemBlockWriter.CreatePreparedBlock` + `BlockPlacement.PlaceDefinitionWithStatus`); **R3** = RACKEDITAR 'Insertar' (the edit chain U-50, then the same batch chain as R2). A row without a route note holds on every route that reaches it. RACKPROYECTAR is not a route of this inventory (AR6-04, section 8).

### 3.2 Operations required by the seam contract (compiled independently; AR2-37)

**Source.** The 203 rows below were compiled by the independent compiler directly from the verbatim contract fragments (V2.1 4.2, 7, 7.1, 15.2..15.7, 19.1, 27.2; V2.2 PA-6, PA-10; V2.4 section 1), with the exact quote of each clause in the evidence file `sa1-compiled-operations.json`. **Binding to the contract identity (BA06-F01):** the fragments are verbatim sections of V2.1 (blob `6259a3be`), V2.2 (blob `fd7260c5`) and V2.4 section 1, which is byte-identical in draft revisions 1, 2 and 3; these documents are members of the composite contract identity of BA-01 (BA-11 registry section 1), and V2.3 and V2.5 contribute no text to the fragments. Check `BA06-C8` verifies that every fragment is contained verbatim in the current contract texts. `R-list` = the V2 rows that express the same operation (the comparator's mapping). `Class` = the operation class of section 3.3: for the 15 `MUTATING` rows (14 map to a class; C-105, SL-3, is excluded by the sealed scope), for the 10 `NON_MUTATING_READ` rows (C-13 is an instrument sample of I-07, not an operation class) and for the guard and result rows that read, open a transaction or hold the lock. Kinds: `MUTATING` 15, `NON_MUTATING_READ` 10, `CHECK_OR_GUARD` 85, `RESULT_OR_REPORT` 50, `PROHIBITION` 43. **V4 amendment:** C-98 (DC-47). **V6 amendment (AR5-03):** C-72.

| id | actor | kind | operation | clause fragments | R-list (V2) | class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C-01 | ORCH | CHECK_OR_GUARD | Acquire and hold the document lock across PREPARE-R, the SL-5 import and every seam write; seams never take the lock themselves | V2.1 15.2-15.7 | R-01 | OC-LOCK (X-01: held from PL to V1) |
| C-02 | ORCH | MUTATING | Start exactly one MUTATION transaction T_M on the document Database's transaction manager. It is the only transaction that holds the logical rack's definitions and references | V2.1 15.2-15.7; V2.1 4.2 | R-02 | OC-TM |
| C-03 | ORCH | RESULT_OR_REPORT | Pass the document Database and the orchestrator's Transaction (T_M) to each creation seam (SL-1, SL-2, SL-4, and SL-3 if authorized) | V2.1 15.2-15.7 | - | - |
| C-04 | ORCH | MUTATING | Commit T_M (the orchestrator is the only committer), and only after the PV checks pass | V2.1 15.2-15.7; V2.1 4.2; V2.2 PA-10 | R-02 | OC-TM |
| C-05 | ORCH | MUTATING | Abort and then dispose T_M whenever a seam call fails (PRE-WRITE or POST-WRITE) or the PV consistency check fails | V2.1 15.2-15.7; V2.1 4.2; V2.2 PA-10 | R-02 | OC-TM |
| C-06 | ORCH/SL-1/SL-2/SL-3/SL-4 | CHECK_OR_GUARD | Expose top-transaction identity only through the typed authority IsTopTransaction(myTransaction), which returns YES/NO/UNKNOWN | V2.1 4.2 | R-03 | OC-TOP-CHECK |
| C-07 | ORCH/SL-1/SL-2/SL-3/SL-4 | CHECK_OR_GUARD | In IsTopTransaction, compare live objects only: the native identity (UnmanagedObject) of the caller's transaction against that of the current top transaction at that instant | V2.1 4.2 | R-03 | OC-TOP-CHECK |
| C-08 | ORCH/SL-1/SL-2/SL-3/SL-4 | PROHIBITION | IsTopTransaction rejects a disposed, null or stale wrapper by returning UNKNOWN (or NO), never YES for a different live transaction | V2.1 4.2 | - | OC-TOP-CHECK |
| C-09 | ORCH/SL-1/SL-2/SL-3/SL-4 | PROHIBITION | IsTopTransaction never compares with a stored value, and the native identity is never an identity the contract stores | V2.1 4.2 | - | OC-TOP-CHECK |
| C-10 | ORCH/SL-1/SL-2/SL-3/SL-4 | PROHIBITION | Never persist the native transaction pointer as a durable identity | V2.1 15.2-15.7; V2.1 4.2 | - | - |
| C-11 | ORCH | PROHIBITION | Never use the native transaction pointer as a log key | V2.1 4.2 | - | - |
| C-12 | ORCH | RESULT_OR_REPORT | Log each identity check as the boolean result plus a sequence number | V2.1 4.2 | - | - |

#### 3.2.1 Disposition of the V2 R-list

The V2 rows R-01..R-21 (the critic's list T3) are superseded by the compiled rows. R-03 is restated on the `IsTopTransaction` authority (BA06-F02); the verdict texts of R-01, R-06, R-14 and R-21 are resolved by the comparator's resolutions (BA06-F06); R-01 is re-resolved in V4 (AR3-22). The comparator's texts cite its own ids DF-01..DF-51: **DF-nn = DC-nn** (same order; check `BA06-C6`).

| V2 row | Disposition |
|---|---|
| R-01 | SUPERSEDED by C-01, C-89, C-120, C-132; sub-claim resolved: REWORD: the orchestrator holds the document lock for PREPARE-R, the SL-5 import and every seam write. Do not assert one continuous lock (DF-01). **V4 RE-RESOLVED (AR3-22):** one document lock, held continuously from PL to V1 (V2.1 3.4 P-L3, 5, 8; AR2-11): X-01. The comparator's "Do not assert one continuous lock" is withdrawn |

#### 3.2.2 Difference log of the contract part (compiled list versus T3 and the R-list)

Columns 2 to 5 are the comparator's output (evidence); column 5 is the comparator's **proposed text**, not inventory text. Column 6 states what V4 did with each difference and where.

| Id | Difference | Resolution (comparator) | Clause (comparator) | Comparator's proposed text (evidence) | V4 disposition |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DC-01 | R-01 leaves out PREPARE-R, which also runs under the lock. It asserts ONE continuous spanning lock, which no fragment states, and does not mention the static guard. Its 'exact wording UNCERTAIN' verdict came from the critic lacking the contract text, and the clauses now resolve it. Covers C-01, C-120 and C-89 (lock part). | REWORD | V2.1 15.3 'no `LockDocument` / forbidden; the orchestrator holds the lock; static guard'; 15.3 'seams write only document-database objects under a held lock'; 15.2 SL-5 '(outside `T_M`, under the lock)'; 15.5 'PREPARE-R computes the expected effective names under the lock' | R-01 (ORCH; SL-1, SL-2, SL-4) The orchestrator holds the document lock for PREPARE-R (expected effective names), for the SL-5 import in PREPARE-W (outside T_M) and for every seam write inside T_M. No seam calls LockDocument (static guard). The fragments do not say whether one continuous lock spans all three phases; record that as open. [V2.1 15.2, 15.3, 15.5] | **RE-RESOLVED (AR3-22):** one document lock held continuously from PL to V1, never released and re-acquired (V2.1 3.4 P-L3; 5 row PL; 8; AR2-11, `NC-E03-REACQUIRE`); on the abort path ABORT-VERIFY reads under the still-held lock (V2.1 11). Applied as X-01 (3.2.5) and class `OC-LOCK` (3.3). The proposed text's "record that as open" is withdrawn |
| DC-02 | R-02 merges three orchestrator transaction operations and one seam prohibition. Start, commit and abort+dispose are separate transaction events with separate E-06 sampling points (PS, PV, post-commit or post-abort), so they need separate rows. R-02 also leaves out the preconditions of Commit (PV checks, PA-10 consistency), the disposal after Abort, the abort triggers, and the static guards. Covers C-02, C-04, C-05, C-117, C-118, C-119 and C-131. | SPLIT | V2.1 15.3 'participation in one `MUTATION` transaction', 'no internal `Commit`/`Abort`', 'no `Dispose` of the caller transaction', 'failure atomicity'; 15.4 POST-WRITE 'the caller must abort'; 4.2 PS, PV and 'after `Commit()` returns, or after `Abort()` and disposal' rows; PA-10 'aborts before `Commit()`' | R-02a (ORCH) Start exactly one MUTATION transaction T_M on the document Database's transaction manager; every definition and every reference of the logical rack is created inside it (PS: count 1, IsTopTransaction(T_M) = YES). R-02b (ORCH) Commit T_M only after the PV checks pass (count 1, no nested transaction, PROTECTED_OBJECTS consistent with the phase-A domain); count 0 after Commit() returns. R-02c (ORCH) On any seam failure (PRE-WRITE or POST-WRITE) or a PV inconsistency, call Abort() and then dispose T_M; count 0 afterwards. R-02d (SL-1, SL-2, SL-4; SL-3 if authorized) No internal Commit, no internal Abort, no Dispose of the caller transaction; each enforced by a static guard. [V2.1 15.3, 15.4, 4.2; V2.2 PA-10] | REFLECTED in C-02, C-04, C-05 (class `OC-TM`), C-117..C-119 and C-131; the E-06 points are C-22..C-25 |
| DC-03 | R-03 prescribes an obsolete mechanism: a direct comparison of TopTransaction.UnmanagedObject with T_M.UnmanagedObject. The contract exposes top-transaction identity only through the typed authority IsTopTransaction(myTransaction) -> YES/NO/UNKNOWN. UnmanagedObject is an internal detail of that authority, compared only between live objects at that instant. The authority must return NO or UNKNOWN, never YES, for a disposed, null or stale wrapper, and must never compare with a stored value, persist the pointer, or use it as a log key. R-03 also omits the consequence: NO or UNKNOWN is the PRE-WRITE failure TransactionMismatch. The R-03 gap column points at RackDefinitionCreator.cs:151-164 (a raw comparison), which is a precedent at most, not the authority. Covers C-06..C-10, C-116 and C-141 (transaction part). | REWORD | V2.1 15.3 caller-owned `Transaction`: 'verifies that it is the live top transaction through the typed authority `IsTopTransaction(myTransaction)` (4.2); a `NO` or `UNKNOWN` is a PRE-WRITE failure. No native pointer is persisted as an identity'; V2.1 4.2 'Typed identity authority' bullets; 15.4 `TransactionMismatch` PRE-WRITE | R-03 (SL-1, SL-2, SL-4; SL-3 if authorized) Before the first write, call the typed authority IsTopTransaction(myTransaction) on the received T_M. Proceed only on YES. NO or UNKNOWN is the PRE-WRITE failure TransactionMismatch, and nothing is written. The authority compares live objects only, at that instant; UnmanagedObject is an internal detail, never stored, never persisted as an identity and never a log key. It returns NO or UNKNOWN, never YES, for a disposed, null or stale wrapper. A raw TopTransaction.UnmanagedObject == T_M.UnmanagedObject comparison is not the contract mechanism. [V2.1 15.3; 4.2; 15.4] | REFLECTED in C-06..C-10, C-116 and C-141 (class `OC-TOP-CHECK` for C-06..C-09 and C-116) and in the R-03 restatement (3.2.1) |
| DC-04 | The BA-06 §5 residual row 'TopTransaction wrapper identity and UnmanagedObject semantics, on which the R-03 check relies' treats the obsolete mechanism as the thing to qualify. #### 3.2.4 Ambiguities recorded by the compiler

These are places where the fragments alone do not determine an operation. **Each is either resolved by a clause outside the fragments, cited in the V4 disposition column, or listed as a contract question for the Architect (section 3.2.6).** None is left to the implemented-seam trace (E2): a dynamic trace cannot decide a contract question (AR3-22).

| Id | Ambiguity (compiler) | V4 disposition |
|---|---|---|
| AMB-01 | Kind taxonomy gap: several required operations fit no kind exactly. These are in-memory composition or supply of values (envelope composition C-126, NewRackId minting C-151, transform supply C-161, passing Database/Transaction C-03), lock holding (C-01), cache invalidation and keying (C-42, C-59), and starting a transaction (C-02). They were classified by the closest kind. | RESOLVED: the kind is the compiler's label, not a contract term. The operations it names are placed by clauses outside the fragments: the lock of C-01 by V2.1 3.4 (P-L3) and 8 (X-01, `OC-LOCK`); the start of `T_M` (C-02) by V2.1 8 (`OC-TM`); C-03, C-126, C-151 and C-161 are orchestrator inputs with no database primitive (V2.1 8: the orchestrator calls the seam with the caller-owned `Database` and `Transaction`); C-42 (in `OC-LIB-ACQ`) and C-59 (a keying rule of that class, with no primitive of its own) belong to the acquisition of the private copy, which V2.1 6 places in PREPARE-R |
| AMB-02 | ORCH/SL-5 split in library acquisition and PREPARE-W is not determined. 15.2 gives SL-5 'a fresh acquire of the library', but 19.1 step 6 and 7.1 say SL-5/the importer 'receives the opened Database', which implies another actor opens it. No actor is assigned to the pre-import re-hash, generation-id revalidation, fingerprinting, effect check or manifest keeping. | RESOLVED in part, and CONTRACT QUESTION CQ-01 for the rest: V2.1 6 (library input identity and cache freshness are PREPARE-R content) and V2.1 5 (row PR: the `LibraryInputRecord` is written at the end of PREPARE-R) place the acquisition and the opening of the library `Database` in PREPARE-R; ALT-21D proposal V4 3.3 item 1 names "the product" as the actor of the pre-import re-observation. This artifact assigns the acquisition, the open and the PREPARE-W checks to the orchestrator and the import to SL-5 (CQ-01) |
| AMB-03 | PA-6 replaces V2.1 19.1 step 2 (the size/last-write stat bracket), yet PA-6 itself, the 19.1 UNKNOWN row ('the acquisition bracket differs') and the safeguards list ('the torn-acquisition bracket (step 2)') still cite step 2. It is unclear whether the stat bracket is still required on top of authority A/B. Step 3 still records 'source stat'. | RESOLVED by V2.2 0.2 (row PA-6: "step 2 and the authority rule replaced") and 0.1 (V2.2 governs over V2.1 for the clauses it names): the size and last-write bracket of step 2 is replaced by authority A or B; the remaining mentions of step 2 (the refusal sentence of PA-6, the 19.1 UNKNOWN row "the acquisition bracket differs", the safeguards list) read as the replaced check. Step 3 is not replaced: the `LibraryInputRecord` still records the source stat |
| AMB-04 | PA-6 authority A vs 19.1 step 1: step 1 opens the source with write-deny only 'when the host allows it'. No preference order or selection rule between authority A and B is given. PA-6 authority A also resembles 19.1 'Alternative A' (allowed 'only through a reviewed amendment'), and the text does not state that PA-6 is that amendment. | RESOLVED by V2.2 0.1 and PA-6: the authority is "one of two", recorded as `ACQUISITION_AUTHORITY`; no preference order is required, and either one, once established, suffices. PA-6 belongs to the Architect's closed, pre-approved errata list (V2.2 0.1), so authority A rests on a reviewed amendment, which is what V2.1 19.1 requires of its "Alternative A" |
| AMB-05 | Section 7 says the manifest lists what PREPARE-W 'added or changed', while 7.1 says MODIFY_PREEXISTING = PROHIBITED and 'PREPARE-W only adds'. #### 3.2.5 Supplementary contract rows (V4; V6: X-05; clauses outside the fragments)

The compiler's input did not contain V2.1 3.4, 5 or 8. These rows add the operations those clauses require (DC-01 re-resolution, AR3-22; `Ops(plan)` of V2.1 27.9, BA06V3-N07). They are implementer text for the Architect's exact delta check. **V6 (AR5-03):** X-05 adds the SL-6 reads of V2.1 10.1 to 10.3, with the bounded re-review of `OC-VERIFY-READ` that AR5-03 orders.

| id | actor | kind | operation | clause (outside the fragments) | origin | class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| X-01 | ORCH | CHECK_OR_GUARD | Acquire `LockDocument` at PL and hold **one** document lock continuously from PL through V1, never released and re-acquired: it spans PREPARE-R, PREPARE-W (the SL-5 import, outside `T_M`), MUTATION (`T_M`) and VERIFY up to V1; on the abort path ABORT-VERIFY reads under the still-held lock | V2.1 3.4 (P-L3); V2.1 5 (PL); V2.1 8; V2.1 11; AR2-11 (`NC-E03-REACQUIRE`) | completes C-01; re-resolves DC-01 (AR3-22) | OC-LOCK |
| X-02 | ORCH | NON_MUTATING_READ | Inside `T_M` at PS: read the source `S` and re-observe the PREPARE outputs; the PREPARE outputs must equal PP, and any difference aborts | V2.1 5 (PS); V2.1 8 | BA06V3-N07 (V2.1 27.9: the PREPARE-independent operations inside `T_M`) | OC-TM-READ |
| X-03 | ORCH | NON_MUTATING_READ | Inside `T_M`: the E-08 baseline observation of the read-set at PS and its re-observation at PV | V2.1 5 (PS, PV) | BA06V3-N07 | OC-TM-READ |
| X-04 | ORCH | NON_MUTATING_READ | Inside `T_M` at PV, after the seam writes: the staged reads `R_pre` and `M_pre` | V2.1 5 (PV); V2.1 8 | BA06V3-N07 | OC-TM-READ |
| X-05 | SL-6 | NON_MUTATING_READ | In VERIFY, pass 1 and pass 2: read every element of the sealed list `VERIFY_ELEMENT_LIST` in a fixed order (each destination sibling definition and reference, the top-level reference included; each authored envelope; the address and grouping of every sibling; the source read-set elements; the consumed NOD entries) and enumerate the containers of `PROTECTED_OBJECTS`, comparing with the expected values of `ORACLE_SPEC`; expected write events: none | V2.1 10.1; V2.1 10.2; V2.1 10.3 | AR5-03 (R2:BA06V4-N01) | OC-VERIFY-READ |

#### 3.2.6 Contract questions and their rulings (AR3-22; decisions section 217)

The cells of columns 1 to 4 are the V4 text, kept verbatim as the record of what was asked and read; column 5 records the Architect's ruling or recorded deferral (AR4-12 for CQ-01 to CQ-07, AR4-01 for CQ-08).

| Id | Question | Source | Reading used in V4 | Ruling or recorded deferral (section 217) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CQ-01 | V2.1 15.2 (row SL-5) and 15.8 (P-4) give SL-5 "a fresh acquire of the library", while V2.1 5 (PR), 6 and 19.1 steps 5-6 put the acquisition and the opening of the library `Database` in PREPARE-R and give SL-5 the opened `Database`. V2.1 7 and 7.1 name no actor for the PREPARE-W checks (pre-import copy re-hash and generation-id revalidation, post-import re-query, fingerprints and effect check, manifest keeping). Does "a fresh acquire" only name the input SL-5 consumes, and are the PREPARE-W checks the orchestrator's? | AMB-02; DC-16; DC-34 | yes to both: acquisition and open in `OC-LIB-ACQ` (orchestrator, PREPARE-R), the checks in `OC-PREP-READ` (orchestrator), the import in `OC-IMPORT` (SL-5) | **ADOPTED** by AR4-12: the reading of column 4 |
| CQ-02 | V2.1 7.1: "PREPARE-W does not start unless the declared mode is on the allow-list", but the mode is declared in the importer's structured result, which exists only after an import. When is the allow-list checked? | AMB-06; DC-45 | the importer declares its cloning mode before the first import (a fixed declaration of the importer) and every structured result re-declares it; PREPARE-W starts only if the declaration is on the allow-list, and a result that declares another mode is the cloning `FAIL` | **ADOPTED** by AR4-12: the reading of column 4 |
| CQ-03 | Three outcomes are not given: (i) a mismatch of the private-copy re-hash before the first import (V2.1 19.1 says "O2 with the residue produced so far", which is empty there; V2.1 17.2 row 3 needs a PREPARE-W write and row 2 does not list the case); (ii) a failed generation-id revalidation before an import; (iii) a mismatch of the post-run re-hash of the copy | AMB-07; DC-40; DC-44 | none adopted: the inventory does not depend on the answer; BA-04 V4 needs it for the expected outcomes of scenarios that exercise these cases | **RULED** by AR4-12 for (i) and (ii): before the first import, **O1** (V2.1 17.2 row 2); after at least one import, O2 or O6 by ABORT-VERIFY. **DEFERRED** by AR4-12 for (iii): no outcome row applies after CP and resolving it needs a reviewed amendment; it blocks no content and no scenario |
| CQ-04 | May a seam start and end a nested transaction inside `T_M`? PV requires "no nested transaction" at sampling time and V2.1 15.3 forbids only internal `Commit`/`Abort`/`Dispose`, `LockDocument` and `OpenCloseTransaction`; a nested transaction between two samples is a declared blind spot (V2.1 4.2) | AMB-11 | no nested transaction is planned in the inventory; one observed in the trace creates a new operation class and reopens the review (V2.4) | **DEFERRED** by AR4-12 to the seam implementation review (EXEC-3, P-1..P-5), with the baseline reading recorded: no nested transaction is planned, and one observed opens a new operation class (V2.4) |
| CQ-05 | E-06: is "own bookkeeping" a mandatory second source in every governed run (V2.1 4.2 UNKNOWN conditions; E6-C5 requires two sources for qualification)? How is the three-valued `IsTopTransaction` result logged, given that "the log records the boolean result"? Who owns the implementation of the typed authority (a shared service or each seam)? | AMB-12; AMB-13; DC-31 | the log records YES, NO or UNKNOWN (UNKNOWN is needed for the 4.2 UNKNOWN condition "IsTopTransaction cannot decide"); the second source and the owner are left to the instrument and seam implementation review | **RULED** by AR4-12 for the logging: the log records the three-valued result (YES, NO or UNKNOWN) with a sequence number. **DEFERRED** by AR4-12 for the rest (whether own bookkeeping is a mandatory second source; who owns the implementation of the typed authority) to the qualification of I-07 (E6-C5) and to the implementation review |
| CQ-06 | Does SL-4 write and read back an envelope, and where is the envelope stored? What must SL-1..SL-4 do to "support" the fresh reread of V2.1 15.7 (15.3 row "fresh reread and comparator support")? | AMB-14; AMB-15; DC-08 | SL-4 writes no envelope: V2.1 10.2 and 25.7 list the envelopes per sibling view, and the product stores the envelope in the extension dictionary of the definition (U-25, U-26); SL-1 and SL-2 support the reread by writing the envelope where `RackBlockData.Read` and `RackBlockFinder.ScanEnvelopes` find it and by returning the handles of 15.4. `OC-ENVELOPE` therefore has no SL-4 instance | **ADOPTED** by AR4-12: the reading of column 4; `OC-ENVELOPE` has no SL-4 instance |

| CQ-08 | AR3-01, by ruling, places only the pre-EVM characterization runs (`E4-LEARN-R1-*`, `LK-*`) on the characterization build. Section 9 proposes the same build for the learning micro-scenarios `EV-L-*`, which run before any EVM exists; that proposal is open question AQ-V4-01. V2.1 27.5 lists a change of the tuple among the changes that require a new model version, review and revalidation. If the learning runs use the characterization build, is its `CHARACTERIZATION_BUILD` identity a tuple change for an EVM that governing validation applies on the product build? | AR3-01; section 9 (proposal); AQ-V4-01 | no, when the characterization build differs from the product build only by harness-only switches; the learning and `REVIEWED` records name both build identities | **MOOT** by AR4-01 (V2.1 27.5): the learning runs `EV-L-*` run on the `PRODUCT_BUILD`, the build of the validation runs `EV-V-*`, so no build difference arises between the learned and the validated model (section 9) |

**Rulings (decisions section 217).** AQ-V4-11 is ruled by AR4-12: CQ-01, CQ-02 and CQ-06 adopt the readings of this artifact; CQ-03, CQ-04, CQ-05 and CQ-07 are ruled or deferred as column 5 states; condition 5 of V2.2 PA-11 for the product part is accepted with three recorded limitations (section 2 row 5). AQ-V4-01 is ruled by AR4-01: CQ-08 is moot, and the build and the controls of the learning runs are fixed (section 9). AQ-V4-14 is ruled by AR4-15: no expectation change, and the residual is stated with the reasons (a) to (e) (section 5). Each deferral names its forum (the seam implementation review EXEC-3 with P-1 to P-5; the qualification of I-07 by E6-C5); `STATIC_REVIEWED` was approved with the deferrals in place (AR4-12), and the inventory stays as approved.

- **Counts (BA06V3-N03).** 21 classes: 16 with a learning micro-scenario and 5 with a reasoned exclusion. 14 of the 15 `MUTATING` compiled rows map to a class; C-105 (SL-3) is excluded by the sealed scope (V2.1 25.7). Every `NON_MUTATING_READ` row has a class, except C-13, an instrument sample of I-07. Checks `BA06-C2`..`BA06-C4` of the published check script (section 14) verify these statements.
- **`OC-ENVELOPE` and SL-4 (BA06V3-N03).** C-127 names SL-1..SL-4. The product path writes no envelope on a reference, so `OC-ENVELOPE` has no SL-4 instance in this inventory; the contract question is CQ-06.
- **`OC-LAYER-PRESENT` and the phase-A domain (AR3-15, D-04).** A reused SL-Y layer is a pre-existing record of the sealed list known at plan time; it belongs to the phase-A domain (BA-08 V6 section 5), so an event on it in phase A is adjudicated against this entry (expected write events: none).
- **V6 (AR6-03; AR5-03).** The I-55 creation site `CreatePreparedBlock` (U-42..U-44) runs the same primitive instances as `T_create` and adds no member to `OC-BT-OPEN` .. `OC-DIM` (D-13 site variant TX-A'); its envelope write relocates the call of U-25 and U-26 (`OC-ENVELOPE`, confirmation); U-45 joins `OC-IMPORT` and U-09 moves to `OC-PREP-READ` (U-03 stays in `OC-IMPORT`); X-05 joins `OC-VERIFY-READ`; U-46, U-47 and U-49 have no class (the D-04 pattern; U-47 is not the typed `IsTopTransaction` authority); U-48 is a dormant watch item inside `T_place` (`OC-REF-TOP`, confirmation); U-50 is the edit path (D-11). Section 3.4 gives the per-class carry.
- **`EV-L-OC-TM-READ`** is new in V4; BA-04 V6 carries it. `EV-L-COMPOSED` checks the composition rule `f(plan)` of section 11 on a full mirror of `FX-1F`.

### 3.4 Per-class `STATIC_REVIEWED` carry table at `95690c28` (record of the review; AR6-03, AR5-01 (ii), AR5-02)

The members are those of 3.3; "changed members" are the U-, C- and X-rows whose cells differ from V5 (check `BA06-C14` lists them with the ruling that orders each change). A class carries `STATIC_REVIEWED` only when the Architect's exact delta check of these bytes shows it without change (AR6-03: "nunca por afirmación"); the last column is this version's proposal, not a decision. ### 4.1 V2 differences (V6: D-01, D-04, D-11 and D-13 restated at `95690c28`; D-17 to D-25 added)

The R-nn cited in this V2 table resolve to compiled rows through section 3.2.1.

RACKPROYECTAR/RPY is **not** an entry of the reused path: it projects linked views of an existing rack and does not create the new logical rack that RACKMIRROR reuses; it is an operation-class precedent only under AR2-38 (section 8, D-25), and no evidence transfers (P-8) |

### 4.3 Recorded consequence: nested effective names (BA06-F11)

The requested nested names restart per plan (`SEL_FRONTAL_<Role>_<n>`, `PLANTA_CAB_<n>`) and the effective names come from `UniqueBlockName` against the block table **at the moment of the call** (evidence `sa3b` F5.1, F5.2). When several frontals are created in one `T_M`, the suffixes therefore depend on the creation order (F5.4, a host fact to be confirmed by the trace). **Rule (`Ops(plan)`, section 11):** the orchestrator calls SL-1 in **ascending `k`**, then SL-2, then SL-4 per view; every seam returns the handles of all objects it created, nested definitions included (C-rows of 15.4), and the EVM selector reads the effective nested names from those handles. Nested names are not a separate contract output (DC log, resolution of R-06).

| residual | affects | why not statically visible |

| That Transaction.Dispose without Commit rolls back, which is the only 'abort' on the creation paths (the edit chain U-50 also calls Abort explicitly); how UNDO groups the separate commits of one command: 3 to 5 on RACKSELECTIVO; on the batch entries, per view, the importer's callee read transactions, T_create' (U-43, U-44), T_place and, when triggered, T_cleanup and T_purge, each an independent committed unit (a later cancel does not erase the views placed before); RACKEDITAR 'Insertar' adds the scan, redraw, purge and rename transactions of U-50 before the batch | U-10, U-27..U-35, U-42..U-45, U-50 | Host transaction and undo semantics |
| The implicit document lock of modal commands (no CommandFlags) and how explicit LockDocument calls nest inside it (V6: also the lock sites `SystemBlockWriter.cs:50`, `RackViewPlacement.cs:74`, `RackNewRackName.cs:35`, `RackSiblingScan.cs:102`, `AutoCadSiblingRedrawPort.cs:23, 31, 39` and the nested lock of `RackBlockRenamer.cs:32`) | U-01, U-28, U-34, U-37, U-42, U-45, U-46, U-50 | Host command runtime |
| Defaults applied on AppendEntity without SetDatabaseDefaults (layer, color, linetype, lineweight, and the DBText text style) for the nested refs, DBText, dimensions and the top-level ref. **V4 (AR3-11):** the attributes that neither the plan, the writer constants nor the I-52 authority determine are characterized by `HDM-CHAR-FX1F`, `HDM-CHAR-FXANN` and `HDM-CHAR-FXDIM` (non-governing, AR3-01 mode; BA-04 V6) and pinned as `HOST_DEFAULT_MAP`, keyed by the consumed context variables (`CLAYER`, `CECOLOR`, `CELTYPE`, `CELTSCALE`, `CELWEIGHT`, `CETRANSPARENCY`, `CPLOTSTYLE`, current text and dimension style) captured in the `PRE_COMMAND_STATE_RECORD`; the ST-13 set of the top-level reference is read from the source reference, not from the map. **V5 (AR4-07):** a fourth characterization scenario, `HDM-CHAR-FXANNBLANK` (fixture `FX-ANN-BLANK`, same mode), records the `SYMBOL_RECORD_LAYER` typed form of the created layer (every field except name and colour) with the key values of the run. For the EVM they stay a residual | U-14, U-19, U-22, U-32 | Host defaults |
| BlockReference constructor defaults (Normal, Rotation, ScaleFactors); the coordinate system of the jig's AcquirePoint (UCS vs WCS) and the resulting real transform | U-29, U-32 | Host defaults and user UCS at run time |
| New BTR defaults (Units, BlockScaling, Explodable) and how they interact with INSUNITS | U-12, U-13 | Host defaults |
| Creation and naming of the anonymous dimension block by RecomputeDimensionBlock; how the DIMVAR overrides are persisted | U-22..U-24, F-15 | Host-internal |
| Anonymous representation blocks and enhanced-block data created by setting DynamicBlockReferenceProperty.Value; whether IsDynamicBlock is false for the plain view def | U-15, C-163 | Host-internal |
| Database.Purge semantics (references from erased owners, anonymous blocks), which decide the real extent of U-35/U-36 | U-35, U-36 | Host-internal |
| Round-trip fidelity of the 255-UTF-16-unit Xrecord chunks (a surrogate pair split at a boundary, per-string storage limits) | U-26, C-127, C-128, C-129 | Host storage behaviour |
| Library cache validity: a replacement file with the same mtime and length goes undetected; file sharing and locking under OpenForReadAndAllShare; the side DB is never disposed at unload (Terminate is empty) | U-04..U-06 | File-system and host state; src/RackCad.Plugin/PluginInitializer.cs:11-13 |
| Run-time data: live block-table contents (which blocks are missing, which suffixes apply); the user-settings library path; catalog contents; UI toggle values; user answers (fondo prompt, jig OK, cancel or Enter; V6: the batch entries have no fondo prompt, the views come from the window's list, and the jig result keeps None, Cancel and Error apart, U-30) | U-04..U-26, U-34..U-36, U-42..U-46 | Data-dependent |
| Whether the interactive prompts (GetInteger, Drag) update DB header system variables such as LASTPOINT | U-29, F-03 | Host-internal |
| Live-identity semantics of the `IsTopTransaction` authority (wrapper identity; reuse of a native pointer after a transaction ends), on which C-06, C-07 and C-116 rely. **V4 (DC-04, BA06-F02):** qualified by E6-C7 and E6-C8 `STALE_WRAPPER_CONTROL` before governing use; E6-C8 records `POINTER_REUSE_OBSERVED` (V2.2 PW-4) | C-06, C-07, C-116 (`OC-TOP-CHECK`) | Host-internal; not statically visible |
| Selectability and extents of the placed ref without a Regen after commit (V6: RACKSELECTIVO performs no Regen; the batch entries place with regen false per view and run one Editor.Regen after the batch when at least one view was placed, U-49) | U-32, U-49, F-17 | Host display pipeline |
| Foreign in-process subscribers (another loaded assembly reacting to database or document events) and registered **overrules** that change an operation's effect (restored from V1, BA06-F10) | all classes | the extract contains no such subscriber, but other loaded code can subscribe; E-09 bounds the overrules that are registered at P0, not their effect |
| **V4 (BA06V3-N08):** event classes of the failure paths: the abort of `T_M` after partial writes, the POST-WRITE failures, a failed or partial SL-5 import and the ABORT-VERIFY reads | C-05, C-99..C-102, C-148..C-150 (`OC-ABV-READ`) | not traced: the dynamic-trace plan covers success paths only (section 7, declared limit (3)). **V5 (AR4-15):** no expectation change in the governing abort scenarios, for the reasons (a) to (e) stated below the table |

Each residual is expected to surface, if it produces events, as an **unexplained event** in learning (V2.1 27.2 step 4), which blocks the freeze of the model until it is explained. The failure-path row surfaces only if a learning run reaches such a path; in validation, an event category the frozen EVM does not explain is `EVM_INCOMPLETE`, which requires a new model version, review and revalidation (V2.2 PA-10). **Ruling AR4-15 (section 217; AQ-V4-14).** The failure-path residual changes no expectation of the 20 governing abort scenarios of BA-04 V6, and no learning variant of the rollback is required, for these reasons: (a) phase A is evaluated at PV over the events from the opening of `T_M` to PV, and in the scenarios whose reach is PV the abort follows that evaluation; (b) the later events, an `Abort()` after PV and the failed `Commit()` of scenario U-6b included, fall in the commit-boundary interval, recorded and not adjudicated (V2.1 9, `NG-19b`); (c) the imports of PREPARE-W and their failures are phase 0, log only; (d) the scenarios whose reach is PS or PW never reach PV, so their phase A is `NOT_EVALUATED` (AR2-25); (e) the ABORT-VERIFY reads run after the abort. The residual is therefore this: the failure-path event classes are neither traced (section 7, declared limit (3)) nor learned, and in the governing abort scenarios they fall in phase 0, in the commit-boundary interval, in a phase A that is not evaluated or in the ABORT-VERIFY reads after the abort; they are recorded there and never adjudicated by E-12 phase A. This artifact records the ruling and decides nothing further.

| UI-01 | EditorDiscardPrompt.confirm (private static Func<string,Window,bool>, NOT readonly) — TIER A. Initialized to ShowMessageBox; rewritten at run time by Substitute (swap) and by the Restore scope's Dispose (restore previous). | UI-52 | RackFlowBedWindow.FloorStroke (static readonly, unfrozen SolidColorBrush) — TIER B. | CONFIRMED |
| UI-53 | RackFlowBedWindow.LabelStroke (static readonly, unfrozen SolidColorBrush) — TIER B. | CONFIRMED |
| UI-54 | RackFlowBedWindow.LabelDefault (static readonly, unfrozen SolidColorBrush) — TIER B. | CONFIRMED |
| UI-55 | RackFlowBedWindow.LabelMuted (static readonly, unfrozen SolidColorBrush) — TIER B. | CONFIRMED |
| UI-56 | RackCad.UI tier-A set is exactly four members: EditorDiscardPrompt.confirm, SelectiveCabeceraHeightPrompt.confirmSevere, SelectiveCabeceraHeightPrompt.showInformative (all written only through test seams with no src caller) and ShellResources.dictionary (write-once lazy). All other mutable-typed statics are tier B. | CONFIRMED |

**Seam-relevant findings.** (1) `BlockLibraryDatabaseCache` (single slot keyed by path, last-write time and length) is **not used by the mirror path**; a warm-up that acquired a different library path would evict and dispose the cached side database (evidence `sa4` CORE-02), so neither the warm-up nor the mirror path may call it (BA-08 V6, BA-09 V6). (2) `JsonRackCatalogProvider.Cache` memoizes the catalog per directory; `RackCatalogLoader` always uses the governed key (CORE-10). **V4 (AR3-16 row 1):** the warm-up does not call `Load()` on a catalog copy; if the `WU-DRY` runs show a load at the first governed read of the catalog, the warm-up warms only the type initializer (empty cache, the same state the window would create) and the dependent assemblies, adding no entry. The catalog folder is the BUILD_BOUND tuple field `CATALOG_FOLDER` (V2.5; AR3-13). (3) `RackLog.sink` is written only through a test seam; in production its effect is file-system only (CORE-13). (4) `UserSettingsStore` holds no tier-A static; file-system side effects only (CORE-14). **V4 (AR3-16 row 4):** the warm-up does not call it; the control plane records the hash of `settings.json` before warm-up step 1 and at CP, and a difference makes the run INVALID. (5) The UI tier-A members above are reached only through test seams or the lazy resource dictionary; the mirror path opens no window, and a `RackCad.UI` load inside the governed window is an E-11M finding (detection control: the loaded-assembly list at the window boundaries of the manifest, BA-07 V6). (6) No static event, thread-static, module initializer or static property with a setter exists in `src`; the only static constructors are three in `RackCad.UI` (evidence `sa4` G-01). (7) **V4 (AR3-16; D-15, D-16):** the warm-up exercises the E-12 handler code also by direct invocation, with warm-up-only instances and sinks, so no counter or buffer of the governed instruments carries into the governed logs (BA-09 V6). The trace dry runs of section 7 and the learning runs of section 9 have no governed window and need no `WU-DRY` (AR3-02). (8) **V6 (`95690c28`; Gate 2, census: `UNCHANGED`):** the lines added by `3375aadb..95690c28` contain no non-readonly static field, static event or static setter; the `BlockLibraryDatabaseCache` callers are unchanged (`BlockLibraryImporter.cs:96`, `AutoCadExternalLibraryBlockQuery.cs:27`); the only new environment input is DEBUG-only (`RACKCAD_DEBUG_FAIL_SIBLING_REDRAW_UNIT`, U-50); `RackLogicalNameAllocator` (U-46) reads the tier-B `RackFrameTemplateCatalog.All`. The table above keeps its counts at `3375aadb`; the census is not re-run at `95690c28`.

## 7. Dynamic-trace plan (conditions 3 and 4; submitted for approval together with `STATIC_REVIEWED`, AR3-22)

**Method (chosen).** Each trace is a **non-governing dry run** on the implemented seams, in a scratch copy of the fixture of its plan-shape class (section 10.2), on the **characterization build** of the exact build (AR3-01: a build of the product with harness-only switches; its identity is the run tuple field `CHARACTERIZATION_BUILD`). The dry runs use the AR3-01 mode: `RECORDED_NOT_ENFORCED` = E-03, E-04, E-12 phase A, E-12 phase C, S-1, S-2, S-3; they reach PV, `Commit()`, V1 and PC; their product outcome is `PROVISIONAL_NON_GOVERNING` (never O1..O7); they depend on no EVM, no `HOST_DEFAULT_MAP` and no K-3. AR3-01 names the pre-EVM characterization runs; the trace dry runs are also non-governing by design (V2.1 21.1, the reading AR3-01 rests on), and their use of the mode is part of this plan, which the Architect approves together with `STATIC_REVIEWED` (AR3-22). They have no governed window, so no `WU-DRY` is planned for them (AR3-02). The `REVIEWED` record names the characterization build and the product build it comes from.

| Source | Role in the trace | Why |
|---|---|---|
| T-1 database event log (E-12 instrument I-10: appended, modified, erased, opened-for-modify, reappended, unappended), recorded, not enforced | **primary** | effects on database objects, including host-internal secondary effects (anonymous blocks, cloned dependencies) |
| T-2 transaction-manager events (`TX_CLASS`, V2.1 14.1) | **primary for transaction boundaries when the characterization of V2.1 14.1 shows they are delivered**; an additional log, never proof (V2.1 4.2) | transaction boundaries per operation instance (E-06 sampling sites) |
| T-2 fallback (BA06V3-N08): the I-07 counter samples at the E-06 sampling points (V2.1 4.2) with the `IsTopTransaction` results | used when `TX_CLASS` events are missing or incomplete in the exact tuple (declared, V2.1 14.1) | boundaries between two samples are then the declared blind spot of V2.1 4.2 |
| T-4 `SCOPED_RECORD_COMPARISON` (BA-05 V5 section 4.1) before and after the dry run. Scope: the read-set records of BA-08 V6 section 6 (members of the `PRE_COMMAND_STATE_RECORD`) plus the `ENUMERATION` records of the covered containers, so an added or removed record shows as a change of its container's enumeration; result per member `EQUAL`, `DIFFERENT`, `ADDED`, `REMOVED` or `UNKNOWN` | **complementary** | net effects without delivered events, inside the declared scope and within the coverage limit of BA-05 V5 section 4.1 item 4 (declared limit (1)) |
| T-3 external managed method-entry tracing | **rejected** | tooling does not exist and it would load code into the governed process |

**Declared limits (condition 2).** (1) **Effects only (BA06-F15):** the sources see effects, not primitive invocations; a primitive with no delivered event and no net effect is invisible to the trace, and so is a net effect with no delivered event that T-4 does not see. T-4 is a `SCOPED_RECORD_COMPARISON` (BA-05 V5 section 4.1), not a full-database fingerprint (BA-05 V5 defines none); its coverage limit, repeated here: only the declared scope (the read-set records of BA-08 V6 section 6 plus the `ENUMERATION` records of the covered containers) and only the fingerprinted fields are observed; a change outside the scope, or confined to what BA-05 V5 section 2.4 does not fingerprint, is **not seen**; a member of a class outside BA-05 V5 section 2.3.1 is `UNKNOWN`, reported as `UNKNOWN` without hiding the other members. (2) **T-2 availability (BA06V3-N08 b):** V2.1 14.1 makes the existence of `TX_CLASS` events a characterization result; without them the boundaries come from the fallback above. (3) **Success paths only (BA06V3-N08 c):** the matrix contains success-path dry runs. The primitives reachable only on failure paths are not traced: the abort of `T_M` after partial writes (C-05), the POST-WRITE failures (C-148..C-150), a failed or partial SL-5 import (C-99..C-101) and the ABORT-VERIFY reads (C-102, `OC-ABV-READ`). They are a residual (section 5). Its consequence for the governing abort scenarios (phase A may meet failure-path events that the frozen EVM does not explain: `EVM_INCOMPLETE`, V2.2 PA-10) is ruled by AR4-15 (section 217; section 5). (4) **Build relation (R1:BA06V4-N03; AR4-12 condition (1)):** the trace observes the characterization build built from the same source SHA as the product build (`PRODUCT_BUILD`). During the trace every harness switch is off, every I-14 writer included, except the `RECORDED_NOT_ENFORCED` handling of the AR3-01 set, which changes control-plane decisions only. The `REVIEWED` record carries both build identities (`CHARACTERIZATION_BUILD` and `PRODUCT_BUILD`) and this attestation. An event class that appears only because of a harness difference is recorded as a finding, not added to the inventory.

**Event class** = the tuple (event kind, object class, owner or container, operation instance). **Reconciliation (BA06V3-N08 a):** every operation instance inside the trace window (P0 to PC of the dry run) is reconciled, the orchestrator's included: the seam calls (SL-1, SL-2, SL-4, SL-5, SL-6) and the orchestrator operations of section 3.3 (`OC-LOCK`, `OC-LIB-ACQ`, `OC-PREP-READ`, `OC-TM`, `OC-TM-READ`, `OC-TOP-CHECK`). Each is reconciled against the instances predicted statically for its plan shape: the U-, C- and X-rows (3.1, 3.2, 3.2.5), `Ops(plan)` for the instances inside `T_M` (section 11), and the PREPARE and VERIFY instances of the same plan. An observed event class with no predicted instance creates a new operation class and **reopens the review** of that class (V2.4); a predicted instance with no observed event is recorded (a class that emits nothing is a fact, not a gap). Events of PREPARE (E-12 phase 0), of the commit boundary (phase B) and of the interval V1..PC are reconciled the same way: that governing runs do not adjudicate them does not exempt them from the trace.

**Coverage matrix.** Rows are the seam entry points, plus one row for the orchestrator operations (not a seam entry point); the columns are the plan-shape classes of section 10.2 (`SH-FN` and `SH-FALL` with 2 and 4 fondos), with the fixtures of 10.2 (`SH-LAY-ADDED`: `FX-ANN-BLANK`, a legacy-product-state fixture built by the declared `FIXTURE_CONSTRUCTION_BUILD`, AR6-02; `FX-SCRATCH` is not a trace fixture; it serves only the learning micro-scenarios `EV-L-OC-LAYER` and `EV-L-OC-TM` (3.3)). Every cell is one dry-run family; the evidence reference is `UNSET` until executed.

| Entry point | SH-F1 | SH-FN (2) | SH-FN (4) | SH-FALL (2) | SH-FALL (4) | SH-P | SH-FP | SH-ANN | SH-DIM | SH-IMP | SH-LAY-ADDED |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SL-1 (per fondo `k`, ascending) | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | n/a | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET |
| SL-2 | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET |
| SL-4 | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET |
| SL-5 (import needed) | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | UNSET | n/a |
| SL-5 (nothing to import) | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | n/a | UNSET |
| SL-6 (read-only verify; expected write events: none) | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET |
| ORCH (not a seam entry point; reconciled in every family: `OC-LOCK`, `OC-LIB-ACQ`, `OC-PREP-READ`, `OC-TM`, `OC-TM-READ`, `OC-TOP-CHECK`) | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET | UNSET |

## 8. `RackDefinitionCreator` (AUTH-15) as an operation-class precedent only (AR2-38; V6: AR6-04)

Evidence `B6` (F1.14, F1.15) and `sa3b` F1.8: the Selective frontal and planta builders return `HeaderRunPlan`, and AUTH-15 overload A executes **the same drawer method** (`LateralHeaderDrawer.CreateSystemBlock`) inside the caller's verified top transaction, with an ordinal envelope read-back and a typed `MissingLibraryBlocks` failure. It is an **operation-class precedent only** (`OC-BTR-*`, `OC-REF-*`, `OC-DYN`, `OC-LAYER`, `OC-TEXT`, `OC-DIM`, `OC-ENVELOPE`); it is **not** an implementation option. At `1304101d` (AUTH-15-C1) the overload no longer requires `Name` in the envelope. **V4 (consistency with BA-08 V4, finding D-02), restated at `95690c28`:** overload A takes a `RackEmbedDocument` (`RackDefinitionCreator.cs:45`), serializes it itself with `RackEmbedStore.Serialize` (`:186`) and writes and reads back its own serialization (`:203`, `:205`), not caller bytes; it does not meet C-127 (write the caller-composed envelope verbatim) either. The blob is `55220527` at `1304101d` and at `95690c28`; at `3375aadb` (blob `f316f7bc`) the lines were `:45`, `:187`, `:204`, `:206` (Gate 2: `STILL_TRUE_WITH_NEW_IDENTITY`).

**V6 (AR6-04; Gate 2 RL-03, N-09; AR5-13).** The V5 sentence "It has **no production caller**" is **superseded at `95690c28`**, and AR3-12 with it: RACKPROYECTAR/RPY (`RackProyectarCommands.cs:21-40`) calls overload A for Selective header-run views (`RackProjectionKindSessions.cs`, the Selective session; `RackProjectionWriteScope.cs:52-60`) inside one caller-owned lock and transaction (`RackProjectionWriteScope.cs:23-37`: `LockDocument` :26, `StartTransaction` :29), with the `TopTransaction.UnmanagedObject` precheck of overload A (`RackDefinitionCreator.cs:156-161`), a model-space reference with a computed position and rotation and no jig (`PlaceReference`, `RackProjectionWriteScope.cs:90-106`), one commit (:108) and discard by dispose (:110-114); its import runs under its own lock before the scope (`RackProjectionCommandPort.cs:106-115`, importer :162). RACKPROYECTAR is a **precedent, not an entry of the reused path** (D-01, D-25): it projects linked views of an existing rack and does not create the new logical rack that RACKMIRROR reuses. It adds no row to section 3.1 and no member to any class of section 3.3; `PlaceReference` is I-55 code, not AUTH-15, and is a separate precedent for `OC-REF-TOP`. **No evidence transfers by family name**: neither the AUTH-15 host evidence nor anything observed on RACKPROYECTAR is evidence for the Selective seams (P-8 remains required). These facts come from the Gate 2 evidence (RELINK-0605, N-09) and a line check of this version at `95690c28`; the blind recompile of section 4.4 does not cover RACKPROYECTAR (RD-01). The code facts of V2.1 15.1 that this section contradicts at `95690c28` (F-2) are re-dated by CT-21D V2.6 (AR6-05; header `PREREQUISITES` (3)).

## 9. Learning-corpus definition

The learning corpus is the set of isolated micro-scenarios of the operation classes of section 3.3 that have one (`EV-L-OC-*`, 16 of them, `EV-L-OC-TM-READ` included), their fixtures and the sessions that ran them, sealed by hash (V2.1 27.3; `LEARNING_CORPUS_HASH`, section 12). The 5 classes with a reasoned exclusion (`OC-LOCK`, `OC-LIB-ACQ`, `OC-PREP-READ`, `OC-TOP-CHECK`, `OC-ABV-READ`) have no micro-scenario; section 3.3 gives the reason. Repetitions: `PARAM-02` (BA-10 V6; UNSET).

- **`EV-L-COMPOSED` is outside the learning corpus (AR4-01; R1:BA06V4-N04).** Learning uses only micro-scenarios that isolate single operations (V2.1 14.3; 27.2 step 2), and the corpus is the set of those micro-scenarios (V2.1 27.3). `EV-L-COMPOSED`, which checks the composition rule `f(plan)` of section 11 on a full composed plan of `FX-1F` in a scratch document, driven by the micro-scenario driver on the `PRODUCT_BUILD`, with no mirror product run (the plan of BA-04 V6; the 3.3 bullet keeps its approved V4 wording, AR4-12), is therefore neither in the learning corpus nor in `LEARNING_CORPUS_HASH`. It is a `NON_GOVERNING_BY_DESIGN` composition check against the `DRAFT` or `UNDER_REVIEW` model (V2.1 27.2 steps 3 to 5): it contributes no model entry, and every event of it that no isolated entry explains is recorded as `UNEXPLAINED_EVENTS` (V2.1 27.2 step 4), which blocks the freeze until it is explained. BA-04 V6 carries its scenario fields; the number of its runs is set by BA-10 V6 section 2.2 (`CHAR_RUNS = N_det + 1` for the composition check, accepted by AR5-08), the pointer that closed question Q-V5-BA04-3 of the BA-04 round V5.
- **Build and controls of the learning runs (AR4-01).** The 16 learning runs `EV-L-OC-*` are **governing** runs of group E12-L on the **`PRODUCT_BUILD`**, the same build as the validation runs `EV-V-*`. The micro-scenario driver is declared as a harness DLL (V2.1 2.1) of the one `PRODUCT_BUILD` profile (one admissible BUILD_BOUND profile per build under test, AR4-06 (1)): it is present in every `PRODUCT_BUILD` run and inert outside `EV-L-*`, so it is present and inert in the validation runs (AR4-01). The learning runs are **not** in the AR3-01 mode. `RECORDED_NOT_ENFORCED` = E-04, E-12 phase A and E-12 phase C, each on its own basis: E-12 phases A and C by V2.1 27.2, 14.3 and V2.2 PA-2 (no `FROZEN` model exists during learning: the model is drafted after the learning micro-scenarios, reviewed and frozen, V2.1 27.2 steps 2 to 5, and only a `FROZEN` model is used by governing validation; E-12 records the event multiset of each micro-scenario, and a micro-scenario that reaches `Commit()` is not aborted by phase A); E-04 by V2.1 3.3 (the admitted set is defined per checkpoint for the designated route of the mirror command, which a micro-scenario is not), not as an extension of AR3-01. E-03 is **enforced** under the `LOCK_MODE` pin; S-1, S-2 and S-3 are `NOT_EVALUATED` (AR2-25); every other control stays fail-closed (V2.1 3.2); `EXPECTED_PRODUCT_OUTCOME` = `NONE`. BA-04 V6 carries this set per scenario; neither artifact defers to the other.
- **CQ-08 is moot (AR4-01; V2.1 27.5).** Learning and validation run on the same build, so the build question of CQ-08 (3.2.6) does not arise.
- **What the AR3-01 mode covers.** By ruling: `E4-LEARN-R1-*` and `LK-*` (AR3-01), `HDM-CHAR-*` (AR3-11; four with `HDM-CHAR-FXANNBLANK`, AR4-07), `WU-DRY-*` (AR3-02) and the `Q-I14-*` vehicles (AR4-01); and, as part of the approved trace plan, the trace dry runs of section 7 (AR4-12 condition (1)). The learning runs are outside it.
- **No `WU-DRY` (AR3-02).** Learning runs are not mirror runs with a governed window; no `WU-DRY` is planned for them (declared).
- **`REVIEWED` before the first `LEARNING` run (V2.4): where the order is shown (R1:BA06V4-N02, R2:BA06V4-N04).** The EVM record does not exist before learning: it is first created, with `EVM_STATUS = DRAFT`, when the model is drafted after the learning micro-scenarios (V2.1 27.2 step 3), and its field `INVENTORY_REVIEW_RECORDS` is written then (section 12); no `FROZEN` model is in use during learning. The order is therefore bound by records that exist at learning time: (1) every learning session record cites the SHA-256 of the `REVIEWED` record, and the sealed learning-corpus record (V2.1 27.3) cites the same hash; (2) the `REVIEW_RECORD_ADDED` entry that custodies the `REVIEWED` record in BA-07 has an index at most the `h` of the run-start anchor (`IN_USE.runStartAnchor`) of every `EV-L-OC-*` run (BA-07 V6 section 6.1 and section 7, U1 to U3). The learning runs are governing (AR4-01) and record their use of the manifest in BA-07 custody; their `IN_USE` entry is appended after the run ends, so its index alone would not show the order, but the run-start anchor it carries is fixed at run start, when the control plane samples the run's BUILD_BOUND fields before library acquisition, and its `h` covers only entries appended before that anchor was made; condition (2) therefore shows that `REVIEWED` was anchored in custody before the run started. A learning run whose session record cites no `REVIEWED` record, or whose run-start anchor does not cover the `REVIEW_RECORD_ADDED` entry of that record, does not meet V2.4 and cannot enter the learning corpus. The EVM field repeats the cited hash; it does not prove the order. Cross-artifact note: BA-07 V6 states this order in section 5.1 ("`REVIEWED` before the first `LEARNING` run": a `REVIEW_RECORD_ADDED` index greater than that `h` is `REVIEW_AFTER_LEARNING_START`) and verifies condition (2) in step 5(c) of its verification algorithm (6.2); the BA-07 reading of the run-start anchor (`IN_USE.runStartAnchor`, kept apart from the per-manifest custody reference) is accepted by AR5-06 (a) (decisions section 220).

## 10. Validation-corpus definition

### 10.1 Rules

Disjoint from learning (different sessions, fresh fixtures); governing only against a `FROZEN(hash)` model (V2.2 PA-2); includes plans structurally outside the learning corpus. **V5 (AR4-01):** validation runs on the same `PRODUCT_BUILD` as learning; the micro-scenario driver is a declared harness DLL of the one `PRODUCT_BUILD` profile (AR4-06 (1)), present in every `PRODUCT_BUILD` run and inert outside `EV-L-*`, so it is present and inert in the validation runs `EV-V-*`. **V6 (AR5-04):** the recorded build equivalence of AR4-06 (4) also covers the reverse direction: `EVM_FROZEN`, learned and validated on the `PRODUCT_BUILD`, applies to a governing run on the `CHARACTERIZATION_BUILD` only when that run's `RUN_PREREQUISITES` hold the `BUILD_EQUIVALENCE_RECORD`, cited in `RUN_START`, whose method shows the assemblies present only in the characterization build event-neutral inside the governed window unless a scenario names them, and compares the `CATALOG_FOLDER` hash sets of both profiles; without that record `EVM_FROZEN` does not apply (the run does not start, or E12A and E12C cannot be PASS). BA-07 V6 extends U5 to that direction and `EA2-DEFERRED-EVENTS` is the catalogue safeguard (BA-04 V6). Learning and validation still run on the `PRODUCT_BUILD`.

### 10.2 Plan-shape classes (final list for the baseline)

| Class | Description | Fixture (BA-08 V6) |
|---|---|---|
| `SH-F1` | one frontal view, single fondo | `FX-1F` |
| `SH-FN` | one frontal view of a multi-fondo rack (2 or 4 fondos) | `FX-2F`, `FX-4F` |
| `SH-FALL` | every frontal view of a multi-fondo rack, SL-1 called for every `k` in one `T_M` | `FX-2F`, `FX-4F` |
| `SH-P` | the planta view | `FX-1F` |
| `SH-FP` | frontal view(s) and planta in one logical rack | `FX-1F`, `FX-2F` |
| `SH-ANN` | annotations present; the annotation layer exists because the source drew annotations | `FX-ANN` |
| `SH-DIM` | dimensions present; the dimension layer exists; anonymous dimension blocks | `FX-DIM` |
| `SH-IMP` | library import needed | `FX-IMP` (construction open, K-8) |
| `SH-LAY-ADDED` | a RackCad layer absent when the seam needs it. **V4 (AR3-15, finding D-01):** reachable as a **legacy product state** with `FX-ANN-BLANK` (V6 wording, AR5-11 and AR6-02, FXB-01..FXB-07: at `95690c28` no path creates a blank-named new Selective rack, every new rack is named `Selectivo N` by I-60, but the exact build keeps a legacy blank name, which RACKEDITAR and the projection do not rename; `FX-ANN-BLANK` is therefore built by the declared `FIXTURE_CONSTRUCTION_BUILD` `69daf03a35c630e453e1d9e98136f128bd0325a4` (`1304101d` + I-55 without I-60), a third build role distinct from the `CHARACTERIZATION_BUILD` and from every build under test, with step 1 by RACKSELECTIVO on that build and the following RACKEDITAR 'Insertar' steps possibly on the exact build, BA-08 V6): the source's logical `Name` is blank, `DrawRackName` is ON, every other annotation switch is OFF and dimensions are `None`, so the source draws no name label and does not create its label layer; the mirror is named "<base> - espejo" (V17, never blank; `I60_NAMING_FOR_RACKMIRROR = NOT_YET_ADOPTED`), draws its label and creates the layer. The dynamic-trace family of this class (section 7) and its validation plans run on `FX-ANN-BLANK`; for SL-Y "added", `FX-SCRATCH` is used only for the isolated learning micro-scenario `EV-L-OC-LAYER` (3.3; `EV-L-OC-TM` also uses it), never for the trace or for validation (BA-08 V6 sections 4.1 and 12.3); the rename or removal of a RackCad layer outside RackCad after the source was drawn stays a disclosed residual (BA-08 V6) | `FX-ANN-BLANK` |

### 10.3 `STRUCTURALLY_OUTSIDE_LEARNING_CORPUS`

A validation plan is structurally outside when, for some dimension of the quantity vector (section 11), its value is not among the learning values (including a value above the maximum seen), or its shape class is not represented in learning. `FX-4F` and `FX-BIG` (BA-08 V6) are the designated structurally-outside validation fixtures.

## 11. `f(plan)` and the quantity vector (final candidate list)

`f(plan)` = the multiset union, over the **ordered** list `Ops(plan)` of the operation instances inside `T_M` (V2.1 27.9: "SL-1 per selected frontal fondo, SL-2 for the planta, SL-4 per view, the envelope writes, and the PREPARE-independent operations inside `T_M`"), of `E(operation class, Q(plan))`, each multiplicity affine in `Q` or tabulated over structurally distinct shapes. **V4 (BA06V3-N07):** `Ops(plan)` includes the orchestrator's operations inside `T_M`, positioned per V2.1 section 8:

1. `OC-TM`: open `T_M` (count 1).
2. At PS: `OC-TM-READ` (source read `S`, re-observation of the PREPARE outputs, E-08 baseline observation; X-02, X-03) and `OC-TOP-CHECK` (C-24).
3. Seam writes, in this order: SL-1 for each selected frontal fondo `k`, ascending; then SL-2 for the planta (0 or 1); then SL-4 once per view. Each seam call starts with `OC-TOP-CHECK` (C-116); each SL-1 and SL-2 call includes its envelope write and read-back (`OC-ENVELOPE`).
4. At PV: `OC-TM-READ` (staged reads `R_pre`, `M_pre`; E-08 re-observation; X-03, X-04), `OC-TOP-CHECK` (C-24) and the seal of `VERIFY_ELEMENT_LIST` and `PROTECTED_OBJECTS`.
5. `OC-TM`: `Commit()` (or `Abort()` and dispose). Events from the end of PV evaluation to V0 fall in the commit boundary (phase B: logged, not adjudicated, V2.1 9); this entry serves the trace and learning, not a phase-A expectation.

The operations outside `T_M` (`OC-LOCK`, `OC-LIB-ACQ`, `OC-PREP-READ`, `OC-IMPORT`: E-12 phase 0, log only) and the verifier (`OC-VERIFY-READ`: phase C, expected write set on `PROTECTED_OBJECTS` empty) are not in `f(plan)`; the trace reconciles them (section 7). The domain of adjudication is V2.2 PA-10, including the pre-existing records of the sealed list known at plan time (the reused SL-Y layers, AR3-15). No quantity is added in V4: `OC-TM-READ` and `OC-TOP-CHECK` are expected to emit no write-class event, and learning measures it (`EV-L-OC-TM-READ`); a non-empty result reopens this list (V2.4).

| `q` | Quantity | Source |
|---|---|---|
| q1 | frontal views of the mirrored rack (fondos) | `SelectiveDepthLayout.Count` (`Application/Systems/Selective/SelectiveDepthLayout.cs:18`) and the mirror plan (the selected source frontal references, V2.1 25.2) |
| q2 | planta views of the mirrored rack: **0 or 1 per logical mirrored rack** | the scope row `SEL-PLANTA` of BA-03 V5 and the mirror plan (BA06-F08: the product `EditSelective` selects every planta definition of the rack, 0..N, evidence `sa3b` F6.1; that is not the domain of q2) |
| q3 | nested header groups per view | `HeaderInstanceGrouper` (frontal: buckets of two or more), `SelectivePlantaBuilder.cs:300` (planta: one per distinct frame) |
| q4 | loose piece instances per view | `LateralHeaderDrawer.cs:70-76` |
| q5 | nested placements per view | `LateralHeaderDrawer.cs:56-68` |
| q6 | annotation texts | `SelectiveAnnotations` |
| q7 | dimensions | `SelectiveDimensions` |
| q8 | dynamic-property applications | `LateralHeaderDrawer.cs:415-449` |
| q9 | distinct required library blocks | `LibraryBlockRequirements.cs:53-80` |
| q10 | layers created (0 to 2) | `LayerHelper.EnsureLayer` callers |
| q11 | envelope chunks (255 characters each) | `RackBlockData.cs:35-39` |
| q12 | top-level references placed (= views) | SL-4 |

## 12. EVM artifact schema (V2.1 27.6; V4: DC-51, AR3-22)

| Field | Content |
|---|---|
| `EVM_ID`, `VERSION`, `HASH` | identity of the model and hash of its canonical serialization |
| `EVM_STATUS` | **V4 (DC-51):** the model-level status `DRAFT`, `UNDER_REVIEW` or `FROZEN(hash)` (V2.1 27.2 step 5); only a `FROZEN` model is used by governing validation, and `E12-V` is evaluated only when `EVM_STATUS = FROZEN` (V2.2 PA-2). The comparator's proposed text calls it MODEL_STATUS |
| `LEARNING_CORPUS_HASH` | hash of the sealed learning corpus |
| `INVENTORY_REVIEW_RECORDS` | **V4 (AR3-22; landing of `V24-N2`):** EVM-level list of the SHA-256 of the `REVIEWED` record (the E2 dynamic trace on the implemented seams) and of every reopened operation-class review record, each also under the custody of BA-07. **V5 (R1:BA06V4-N02, R2:BA06V4-N04):** it is written when the EVM record is first created (`EVM_STATUS = DRAFT`, V2.1 27.2 step 3, after the learning micro-scenarios), with the hash of the `REVIEWED` record that was in BA-07 custody (`REVIEW_RECORD_ADDED`) before the first `LEARNING` run, and it gains an entry for every reopened class review. It does not show the order "`REVIEWED` before the first `LEARNING` run" (V2.4); the learning records and the BA-07 run-start anchors show it: the `REVIEW_RECORD_ADDED` entry has an index at most the `h` of the run-start anchor (`IN_USE.runStartAnchor`) of every `EV-L-OC-*` run (section 9, item (2); BA-07 V6 section 7). The `EVM_FROZEN` pin record cites this field |
| `ENTRY_ID` | identity of one entry |
| `OPERATION_CLASS` | a class of section 3.3 |
| `EXPECTED_EVENT_SEQUENCE/SET` | multiset of (event kind, target selector, step) with multiplicity as a function of `Q`, and the partial order if any |
| `EXPLANATION_TYPE` | `PRODUCT_OPERATION`, `HOST_DOCUMENTED` or `HOST_OBSERVED_STABLE` (only as companion behaviour of a product operation, with evidence, citation, Architect review and risk label; never a free-standing permission) |
| `AUTHORITY/CITATION` | the operation, the documentation citation, or the micro-scenarios |
| `RAW_EVIDENCE_REF` | raw event logs |
| `RISK_LABEL` | `NONE` or the declared risk |
| `REVIEW_STATUS` | per entry: `DRAFT`, `UNDER_REVIEW`, `ACCEPTED`, `REJECTED`, with the **reviewer role** that set it (V2.1 27.6; BA06-F16) |

## 16. Corrections not applied in V5 (AR4-12 condition (2))

*Historical record of round V5, kept verbatim. V6 applies the three corrections by ruling AR5-03 (section 19).*

AR4-12 approved `STATIC_REVIEWED` on the V4 bytes and requires the candidate version to show the inventory and the trace plan without content change. Three confirmed minor findings are not applied in V5, and every correction they ask for lies inside the frozen span of sections 3 to 3.2.5 and 3.3, which check `BA06-C13` compares with V4. Most of them change inventory content: the marked amendment of the compiled row C-72 (R1:BA06V4-N01), the supplementary row X-05 (R2:BA06V4-N01) and the class membership of U-03 and U-09 (R2:BA06V4-N02). Two do not: the reword of the DC-41 disposition (the alternative that R1:BA06V4-N01 offers) and the correction of the DC-18 disposition (R2:BA06V4-N01) change only the difference log 3.2.2, which the section 3 "Composition" paragraph classes as a record of the review, not as an inventory row; they are withheld because 3.2.2 lies inside that frozen span. The three corrections are open Architect question AQ-V5-03 (section 16.1): the Architect can record each correction in a ruling, or order it into a later BA-06 version with a re-review of the affected class (AR4-12 condition (2)); whether an edit of a review record inside the frozen span keeps the carry-over belongs to AQ-V5-07. Until then the inventory and the trace plan stay as approved, and the notes of the last column are not inventory text.

| Finding | Correction withheld (AQ-V5-03) and the part of the artifact it changes | Affected class | Note until the Architect decides |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1:BA06V4-N01 | give the compiled row C-72 a marked amendment on the model of C-98 ("captured once by the orchestrator in PREPARE-R at PR, inside the command after PL"; V2.1 5, 6; AMB-09): an inventory row (3.2); or reword the DC-41 disposition to say that C-72 keeps the compiled wording and reads per AMB-09: a review record (3.2.2), inside the frozen span | `OC-PREP-READ` | C-72 keeps its compiled wording ("before the command"). Its capture point is the one that the DC-41 disposition and AMB-09 already state: once, by the orchestrator, in PREPARE-R at PR, inside the command after PL (V2.1 5, 6; ALT-21D proposal V4 3.1). The class placement of C-72 is unaffected |
| R2:BA06V4-N01 | correct the DC-18 disposition ("no reference or transform check is added (V2.1 15.7 has no such clause)"): the top-level reference is a sealed-list element that the verifier reads and compares in pass 1 and pass 2 against a value that the ORACLE_SPEC of BA-08 V5 computes independently of the writer and of `mu_k` (V2.1 10.1, 10.2): a review record (3.2.2), inside the frozen span; and add a supplementary row X-05 for the SL-6 pass-1 and pass-2 reads of every sealed-list element and container enumeration (V2.1 10.1 to 10.3; class `OC-VERIFY-READ`; expected write events: none): an inventory row (3.2.5) | `OC-VERIFY-READ` | the confirmed finding shows the DC-18 disposition to be contrary to V2.1 10.1 and 10.2; those contract clauses govern the VERIFY reads, and the text of DC-18 stays as approved until the Architect decides.
